# Third-Party Licenses

DiffEverything itself is licensed under the MIT License; see [LICENSE](LICENSE).

This file lists third-party Rust crates in the workspace dependency graph and reproduces the license texts published with those crates. It covers normal, build and test dependencies to be conservative.

- Generated: 2026-11-27
- Cargo.lock SHA-256 (first 16 hex): `a1dffdfff9ada75c`
- Third-party packages: 566
- Distinct license texts: 332

The table shows the SPDX expression declared by each crate. Where a crate offers multiple options, DiffEverything relies on a permissive option (MIT, Apache-2.0 or BSD) and only the corresponding license text applies to this distribution.

No GPL or AGPL code is present. The only dependency offering an LGPL option is `r-efi`, which is used under MIT/Apache-2.0.

## MPL-2.0 Source Notice

The dependency graph contains MPL-2.0 code. MPL-2.0-only crates are used unmodified; their source is available from crates.io and the repositories below.

- MPL-2.0 only: `cssparser 0.38.0` — https://crates.io/crates/cssparser/0.38.0 — https://github.com/servo/rust-cssparser
- MPL-2.0 only: `cssparser-macros 0.7.1` — https://crates.io/crates/cssparser-macros/0.7.1 — https://github.com/servo/rust-cssparser
- MPL-2.0 only: `dtoa-short 0.3.5` — https://crates.io/crates/dtoa-short/0.3.5 — https://github.com/upsuper/dtoa-short
- MPL-2.0 only: `selectors 0.41.0` — https://crates.io/crates/selectors/0.41.0 — https://github.com/servo/stylo

Crates that offer MPL-2.0 alongside a permissive option are used under the permissive option:

- `termina 0.3.3` (`MIT OR MPL-2.0`) — https://github.com/helix-editor/termina
